*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Atari Pong游戏中系统评估了五个视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM），发现这些模型在闭环rollout诊断和像素空间零样本MBRL中存在严重缺陷（如球消失、运动错误等），并提出概念引导的空间正则化（CGSReg）作为辅助损失来改善世界模型的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的组件被整体评估，而世界模型本身的建模质量很少被单独研究。作者希望填补这一空白，单独评估世界模型的能力和缺陷。

Method: 1) 复现五个代表性世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）的训练流程并匹配原始智能体性能；2) 冻结学习到的世界模型，通过闭环rollout诊断（使用单独训练的策略与冻结模型交互）检查视觉和动力学错误；3) 进行像素空间零样本MBRL评估（在冻结模型内训练新策略后在真实环境测试）；4) 提出CGSReg（概念引导的空间正则化），对分割出的概念区域施加额外的像素重建损失。

Result: 1) 所有五个模型的rollout都存在明显失败（球消失、错误运动、无效球拍交互）；2) 像素空间零样本MBRL中所有模型策略性能大幅下降，DreamerV3的平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9（接近最低分-21）；3) CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER上改善了闭环rollout和零样本MBRL，但在其他模型和评估指标上效果不一。

Conclusion: 现有视觉世界模型存在严重的建模缺陷，特别是对任务关键概念（如Pong中的球）的建模不足。CGSReg能部分缓解这些问题，但仅靠它还不足以解决所有世界模型的瓶颈。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [133] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能作为自主参与者进入科学研究循环所引发的工业化转型，指出研究正从"工匠模式"转向"流水线模式"，并以美国能源部Genesis Mission为例，系统分析了这一转变带来的七个根本性问题，包括科学能力代际传承的侵蚀、AI理论的不透明性、同行评议的崩溃、范式颠覆性发现的未证实能力、科研议程被政治和工业力量俘获、闭环流水线中系统性错误的累积，以及全球科研共同体的结构性分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI驱动的科学研究所引发的根本性转变——即从以研究者为知识、方法和判断载体的手工艺模式，转向将研究步骤分解、自动化和监督的工业化流水线模式，并探讨这一转变带来的深层次挑战。

Method: 以美国能源部Genesis Mission为典型案例，通过概念分析和理论反思，系统梳理和论述AI工业化科学研究过程中出现的七个关键问题。

Result: 识别并阐明了七个关键问题：科学能力代际传承的侵蚀、AI生成理论的不透明性、机器生成产出泛滥导致的同行评议崩溃、AI在范式颠覆性发现方面的能力未经验证、政治和工业力量对科研议程的俘获、闭环流水线中系统性错误的累积效应、以及全球科研共同体结构性分化为不可通约的两个层级。

Conclusion: 这些问题并非反对AI驱动科学的论据（其潜力是真实且重要的），而是负责任地追求这一潜力所需要正视和应对的前提条件。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research - not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science - whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [134] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench 是一个由临床医生构建的医学AI安全基准，通过44个标注严重等级（1-5级）和安全门类型的合成案例，系统评估AI模型在医疗场景中的失败模式，而非仅衡量其回答正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准仅关注模型是否给出正确答案，未系统评估模型在何种安全边界上失效（如遗漏紧急升级、不安全的远程给药等），缺乏对AI医疗错误模式的细粒度分类和严重性标注。

Method: 由临床医生构建合成基准测试集和失败图谱，标注每个案例的严重性等级（1-5级）和安全门类型（紧急升级遗漏、不安全远程给药、不安全出院安抚、证据编造、不安全协议执行、来源支持缺口）。当前版本（v0.2.1）包含44个经临床医生审查的合成案例及严重性标注、HuggingFace排行榜预览、安全门分类法、临床严重性评分标准和自动化模型响应筛查流水线。

Result: 发布了包含44个合成案例、严重性标注、安全门分类法、评分标准和自动化流水线的公开基准，不含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Conclusion: MedFailBench提供了一种系统评估医学AI安全失效模式的新范式，通过细粒度安全门分类和严重性标注，帮助识别和防范关键临床风险，为构建更安全的医学AI系统提供评估工具。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [135] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过科学可视化素养评估测试（包含49道题、18个可视化图表、8种技术和11种任务类型），系统评测了6个多模态大语言模型（3个闭源+3个开源），并与485名人类参与者对比。结果显示：Gemini表现最强，超过人类平均水平；开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插画、搜索和空间理解任务上表现良好，但在纹理/集成类可视化及定量估算任务上表现不佳。错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估算、流向解释和编码解释方面的反复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对多模态大语言模型在可视化理解方面的评估主要集中于图表类可视化，缺乏对其科学可视化理解能力的系统性评测，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用科学可视化素养评估测试（共49题，覆盖18个科学可视化图表、8种可视化技术和11种任务类型），在封闭世界协议下评测3个闭源模型（如GPT-4系列、Gemini）和3个开源模型（如LLaVA），并与485名人类受试者的数据进行对比分析，同时进行错误分析。

Result: Gemini整体表现最强，超越人类平均水平；开源模型低于人类基线。模型在科学插画、搜索和空间理解任务上表现较好，但在纹理/集成类可视化及定量估算任务上表现差。错误分析显示模型在细粒度定量估算、流向解释和编码解释方面反复失败。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型不具备统一的科学可视化素养，能力在不同技术和任务类型上分布极不均匀。科学可视化素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的一个必要基准维度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [136] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了AI基准测试中项目反应理论（IRT）模型在数据分布与人类测试不一致时的可靠性问题，通过六个LLM基准测试数据模拟18000种条件，发现传统估计器在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在小规模或非正态分布模型集时产生不可靠推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试越来越多地使用IRT模型进行能力估计、系统排名和示例选择，但AI基准数据（少量模型、大量项目、能力分布偏斜/聚类/多峰）与人类测试的数据模式存在显著差异，这种"数据体制不匹配"可能威胁IRT建模的可靠性，需要系统评估。

Method: 从六个广泛使用的LLM基准测试中提取项目参数和能力分布，在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具（边际最大似然估计、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断、神经伪孪生估计器）。在18000个模拟条件下评估计算可行性、可扩展性和推断可靠性（模型排名、预测性能、项目特征）。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准测试场景下变得不可行；可扩展估计器在模型集规模小或能力分布非正态时，会产生不可靠的项目级和排名推断。

Conclusion: 研究识别了潜在特质模型何时能可靠支持AI基准测试、何时会扭曲结论，并提出了可信使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [137] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个基于计划的可视化GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划外部化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件，使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI自动化代理在真实环境中面临动态布局、意外对话框等挑战，且其规划和适应过程通常是内部的，限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构（planner-executor architecture），将任务计划和重新规划外部化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件，支持显式监督、局部修正、自然语言指导和截图交互。

Result: 许多自主GUI代理的失败在计划可见且干预可局部化时是可以结构性修复的；显式重新规划有助于提高GUI自动化的透明性、可控性和适应性。

Conclusion: Plover通过外化计划与重规划过程，使GUI自动化系统更透明、可控和适应性强，显著提升了用户对系统行为的监督和修正能力。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [138] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自进化、专家在环的MDD注释框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，生成符合DSM-5-TR标准、可解释且可审计的精神健康数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集的标注缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了数据透明度和模型可解释性。

Method: 三阶段框架：候选证据选择、标准级DSM-5-TR分析、案例级综合；双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈；输出标注、临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史。

Result: 在专家评审的试点研究中，该方法提高了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效支持构建可解释、符合DSM-5-TR标准的MDD数据集，多轮反馈循环的评估留作未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [139] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中内容危险（文本层面的有害内容）与物理危险（指令在物理世界中执行带来的危险）在模型表征层面是可分离的信号。基于此发现，提出PRISM探针（单层L2正则化逻辑回归分类器），在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率、11.7-13.7%假阳性率，远优于同规模LLM直接判断（24.7-39.0%假阳性率）。同时构建了不含直接伤害关键词的物理安全对比基准PSB-1K（1000对），PRISM达到99.6%准确率和0.7%假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐主要关注文本层面的内容安全（如有害内容生成），但当LLM作为具身智能体的高层规划器时，语言上无害的指令在物理世界中执行可能带来实际危险。这种物理层面的安全风险与传统的文本内容安全是否为同一问题尚不明确，亟需研究这两种危险在模型表征中的关系并开发针对物理安全的检测方法。

Method: 1) 通过隐状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，证明内容危险与物理危险在LLM表征中是可分离的信号；2) 提出PRISM：在全隐状态上训练单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针；3) 构建PSB-1K对比基准（1000对不含直接伤害关键词的物理风险样本）；4) 在Qwen2.5系列（3B/7B/14B/32B）、Phi-3.5、SmolLM2等多种模型上验证。

Result: SafeAgentBench上PRISM达86.2-87.7%准确率、11.7-13.7%假阳性率；同规模LLM直接判断假阳性率为24.7-39.0%。PSB-1K上PRISM达99.6%准确率、0.7%假阳性率，而Qwen2.5-3B判断器拒绝了67.8%的安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上同样有效。

Conclusion: 内容危险与物理危险在LLM表征中是可分离的两种信号。基于隐状态探针的方法（如PRISM）能够有效检测物理层面的危险，超越传统文本内容审核方法，为具身智能体的物理安全提供了一种表征级别的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [140] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，用于自动化元分析。给定自然语言研究问题，它能自动完成搜索策略制定、文献检索、研究筛选、全文评估、数据提取、效应量计算和随机效应元分析，并支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终生成符合PRISMA指南的报告。测试表明其合并效应估计值与专家手动元分析高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据综合（元分析）对于将初级研究转化为可靠知识至关重要，但当前过程主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化推广。

Method: 采用端到端多智能体系统架构，自动完成以下流程：从自然语言研究问题出发，制定搜索策略→检索科学文献→筛选候选研究→评估全文合格性→提取定量统计数据→计算标准化效应量→执行随机效应元分析；此外还支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终输出符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: 系统筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量主张；其产生的合并效应估计值与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g值高度接近。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据综合更具可扩展性，从而支持跨学科的证据决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [141] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过公共讨论界面（如论坛评论区）向预训练语料注入恶意内容的可行性，并提出了HalfLife分析框架用于评估对抗性内容是否被网络爬虫纳入训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对预训练数据投毒的研究局限于Wikipedia等受控数据源，忽视了真实预训练语料的大规模、异构性以及数据筛选管线的影响。本文探索了在更大规模、更现实的网络数据环境下实施投毒攻击的可能性。

Method: 提出HalfLife分析框架，用于评估网络爬取数据的预训练语料中是否包含通过公共讨论界面注入的恶意内容，并基于该框架分析大规模投毒的可行性。

Result: 证实通过公共讨论界面（第三方网页内容）进行的投毒攻击在真实规模的网络爬取数据中是可行的，HalfLife能够有效评估恶意内容的纳入情况。

Conclusion: 第三方网页内容可作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量，在预训练数据投毒的研究中需考虑数据筛选管线与恶意内容纳入的交互影响。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


### [142] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个用于城市驾驶数据探索性因果分析的系统，能够从行车记录仪标注中构建事件表，结合因果结构学习、稳定性检查和效应估计，将自然语言问题映射为因果查询，并生成包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的"Causal Card"，旨在辅助专家生成假设和推理，而非提供确定的因果结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构可获得大量视频数据，但这些数据多为观察性数据且无干预，难以回答因果问题（如"雨水如何改变交通密度"），现有方法缺乏对这类数据的系统性因果分析工具。

Method: 从行车记录仪标注构建结构化事件表，结合PC算法进行因果结构学习、bootstrap稳定性检查，以及基于线性回归和DoWhy的查询特定效应估计；通过schema感知的大语言模型将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询。

Result: 在BDD交通事件案例研究中，系统能够揭示天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的合理关系，并明确展示了不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe被设计为一种假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果主张的来源。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [143] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS是一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将隐式搜索进度转化为显式持久共享状态，解决了工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中容易陷入重复搜索循环的问题，在WideSearch和GISA基准上领先所有评估基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中难以追踪任务进度，搜索失败时单/多智能体系统容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并损害最终输出质量。

Method: 提出SearchOS框架：1) 将开放域信息搜索建模为带引用的关系模式补全；2) 设计面向搜索的上下文管理(SOCM)，将状态外化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆；3) 采用流水线并行调度机制重叠执行子智能体；4) 引入搜索工具中间件控制层拦截模型与工具交互，并设计可复用的分层技能系统。

Result: 在WideSearch和GISA基准上，SearchOS在所有评估指标上领先于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS通过显式状态管理和智能调度机制，有效解决了信息搜索中的重复循环问题，为鲁棒的信息搜索协作铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Campaign Diagrams: Visualizing the March Through the Phases of a Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15225)
*Toluwanimi O. Odemuyiwa,John D. Owens,Michael Pellauer,Joel S. Emer*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出"campaign diagrams"可视化技术，用于现代工作负载中资源利用和瓶颈的阶段级分析，在单一图中同时展示计算吞吐量、内存带宽利用、计算和内存流量以及延迟，通过两个案例（低秩GEMM和Mamba）证明了其发现优化机会的能力优于传统的roofline模型和profiler。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具存在权衡：roofline模型将工作负载聚合成单一点，丢失了所有时间信息；而profiler和trace暴露细粒度事件但掩盖了性能瓶颈所在。需要一种能够在阶段级别同时展示资源利用和瓶颈的可视化技术。

Method: 提出campaign diagrams可视化技术，在单一图中描绘计算吞吐量、内存带宽利用率、计算和内存流量以及延迟。可从分析模型、仿真或profiling数据生成，同时捕获理想边界和实际性能。

Result: 两个案例研究：1)低秩GEMM中揭示了反直觉的结论——降低运算强度可以提升端到端性能；2)Mamba中暴露了跨阶段的融合和流水线优化机会。在这两种情况下，该可视化技术发现了仅靠roofline或profiler难以识别的优化机会。

Conclusion: Campaign diagrams是一种有效的阶段级可视化技术，能够弥补roofline模型和profiler之间的差距，帮助发现新的优化机会。

Abstract: We present campaign diagrams, a visualization technique for phase-level analysis of resource utilization and bottlenecks in modern workloads. Existing tools have a trade-off: rooflines aggregate a workload into a single point and lose all notion of time, while profilers and traces expose fine-grained events but obscure what bounds performance. Instead, a campaign diagram depicts compute throughput and memory bandwidth utilization, compute and memory traffic volume, and latency in a single figure. Since they can be generated from analytical models, simulations, or profiling data, campaign diagrams capture both ideal bounds and a kernel's achieved performance. We demonstrate them on two case studies: a low-rank GEMM, where they reveal the counterintuitive result that reducing operational intensity can improve end-to-end performance, and Mamba, where they expose fusion and pipelining opportunities across phases. In both cases, our visualization technique reveals optimization opportunities that are difficult to identify with rooflines or profilers alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Position: Explainability Research Must Prioritize Foundations over Ad-hoc Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14123)
*Michal Moshkovitz,Suraj Srinivas,Lesia Semenova,Nave Frost,Cyrus Rashtchian,Valentyn Boreiko,Shichang Zhang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Cynthia Rudin,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出可解释AI（XAI）研究虽方法众多，但缺乏将解释集成到端到端、人在回路系统中的方法论，导致解释难以指导实际决策。作者通过分析顶会论文和从业者调查，呼吁学界从开发临时性XAI方法转向解决基础性结构挑战（问题定义不清、评估目标不明确、缺乏反馈管线），并提出了转向以人为中心、可行动为导向的实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法虽然数量激增，但很少真正融入实际工作流程，解释往往被生成后就被丢弃，无法引导有意义的行动。这表明研究存在根本性缺陷：尚未建立将解释集成到端到端、人在回路系统中的方法论。

Method: 对近期ICML、NeurIPS和ICLR论文进行分析，同时开展XAI从业者调查，揭示阻碍累积性进展的反复出现的问题。

Result: 发现XAI领域存在三大基础性结构挑战：问题定义不清晰、评估目标不明确、缺乏解释驱动的反馈管线。

Conclusion: XAI研究应从开发临时性方法转向解决基础性结构挑战，并给出了一个实践清单，旨在推动XAI转向更以人为中心、以行动为导向的范式，为将解释集成到可行动的、反馈驱动的AI系统中提供路线图。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of Explainable AI (XAI) techniques -- from feature attributions to sparse autoencoders -- explanations rarely influence real-world workflows. In practice, they are often generated and discarded without guiding meaningful action. This gap reflects foundational shortcomings: research has not yet established methodologies for integrating explanations into end-to-end, human-in-the-loop systems. This position paper argues that the machine learning community must pivot from ad-hoc XAI methods toward addressing foundational & structural challenges, including unclear problem formulations, underspecified evaluation objectives, and the absence of pipelines for explanation-driven feedback. We support this claim through an analysis of recent ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR papers and a survey of XAI practitioners, revealing recurring issues that limit cumulative progress. We conclude by outlining a practical checklist designed to shift XAI toward a more human-centered, action-oriented paradigm. By emphasizing foundational clarity over the development of ad-hoc methods, we hope to provide a roadmap for integrating explanations into actionable, feedback-driven AI systems.

</details>


### [146] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong,Zesheng Ye,Jianzhong Qi,Lei Feng,Feng Liu,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARPRT方法，通过捕捉提示词与类别之间的依赖关系，为每个类别自适应地分配提示词权重，以无训练方式提升零样本图像分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本分类中的提示集成方法为所有类别共享相同的提示权重向量，忽略了提示词对不同类别的适配性差异（如"aerial view"适合"airport"但不适合"apple"），导致性能受限。

Method: CARPRT采用无训练方式，对每个类别标签和每个可用提示，通过计算在该提示下被预测为该类别的图像的图文相关性得分的平均值，量化提示与类别的专属相关性，再经归一化得到类别特定的提示权重。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准上，CARPRT优于现有类别无关的提示重加权方法，证实了建模提示-类别依赖关系对零样本预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 建模提示词与类别间的条件依赖关系对于零样本预测及更广泛的基于提示集成的VLM应用至关重要，CARPRT是一种有效的无训练解决方案。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CARPRT.

</details>


### [147] [Explainable Geospatial AI for Satellite Ground Station Siting Using LiDAR-Derived Terrain Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14127)
*Shohini Sarkar,Smithi Mahendran,Rishi Chudasama,Varun Mannam,Arav Luthra,Yuvraj Rekhi,Vivek Nadig,Arsh Goenka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于开放地理空间数据的可解释机器学习框架，用于预测代表性杂波高度（RCH），替代ITU-R P.452-18中固定杂波高度的方法。模型使用LiDAR导出的标签训练，选择LightGBM作为回归器，最终MAE为1.79m，R²=0.765，相比ITU基线误差减少超60%，并通过SHAP分析验证了物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ITU-R P.452-18中基于土地利用类别的固定杂波高度忽略了同类地物内部的变异，导致保守的排除区和低地球轨道地面站选址及频谱协调中的站点排名不佳，亟需更精确、可规模化且可解释的RCH预测方法。

Method: 利用USGS 3D Elevation Program的LiDAR数据生成第75百分位杂波高度标签，整合全球土地覆盖、地形、人口、热红外和光学遥感等多源开放地理空间数据作为特征，比较多种回归模型后选择LightGBM，并通过SHAP进行特征归因分析。

Result: 最终模型MAE为1.79米，R²=0.765，相比ITU基线绝对误差降低超60%。SHAP分析表明树冠覆盖度、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率是最重要的预测因子。国际验证和消融实验进一步证实了模型的可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 开放地理空间数据可以在不牺牲可解释性和可部署性的前提下，规模化改进杂波建模，提升低轨卫星地面站选址和频谱协调的精度。

Abstract: Representative clutter height (RCH) is a key parameter in radio propagation and interference analysis because it captures the dominant height of local obstructions that drive terminal clutter loss. Current practice often relies on fixed clutter heights assigned to land use classes in Recommendation ITU-R P.452-18, but this misses within class variation and can lead to conservative exclusion zones and poor site ranking for low Earth orbit ground station siting and spectrum coordination. We present an interpretable, globally deployable machine learning framework for predicting RCH from open geospatial data. The model is trained using LiDAR derived labels from the U.S. Geological Survey 3D Elevation Program and inference time features from global land-cover, terrain, demographic, thermal, and optical remote sensing products. We define RCH using a robust 75th percentile clutter height statistic, evaluate multiple regressors, and select LightGBM for its accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with feature attribution analysis. The final model achieves a mean absolute error of 1.79m and an R^2=0.765, reducing absolute error by more than 60% relative to the ITU baseline. Beyond aggregate fit, we evaluate domain facing criteria relevant to RF planning, including meter scale error, tolerance band accuracy, over and under estimation tails, agreement with ITU clutter height regimes, and SHAP-based physical plausibility. SHAP identifies tree canopy cover, land-cover semantics, and spectral reflectance as the most influential predictors. Studies on segmentation derived features, non-forest ablations, and land-cover matched international validation show that open geospatial data can improve clutter modeling at scale without sacrificing interpretability or deployability.

</details>


### [148] [Certified Domain Consistency for Multi-Domain Retrieval: Label-Free Per-Domain Contamination Control with Conformal Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14157)
*Jayakumar Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: C3R 是一个即插即用的控制层，通过域后验推断和两阶段拆分方案，在推理时无需查询标签即可按域认证污染预算，对最难域保证污染降低而非紧界，经验证从未违反证书而边际控制在每次实验中均违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合多域语料库检索时，常返回相关但错误域的 evidence，现有排序指标无法捕捉此类错域污染，且 conformal 风险控制边界仅能边际覆盖，对最差域覆盖不足。

Method: 提出 C3R，基于两阶段拆分方案和风险控制预测集，构建从推断域到真实域的有限样本迁移界，支持异构预算，可逆推用于部署。核心是从域后验推断出发，在可行时按域认证污染预算，否则弃权。

Result: 在千次重采样校准中，证书从未违反（稳定性结果），而边际控制在每次实验中都违反污染最严重的域；软降级在同等认证污染下比最强的校准级联保留更多召回率。方法在公开测试床（包括联邦法规独立数据集）上可复现。

Conclusion: C3R 是一种冻结栈、重排序器无关的控制层，可有效按域约束污染，LLM 下游探针表明错误权威归因随污染上升，在控制下下降。

Abstract: Retrieval over corpora that mix several domains often returns relevant but wrong-domain evidence that ranking metrics miss and that conformal risk control bounds only marginally, under-covering the worst domains. This work introduces C3R, a drop-in control layer that, from an inferred domain posterior and no query-time label, certifies a per-domain contamination budget where feasible and otherwise abstains rather than silently violating; on the hardest domains it guarantees a reduction, not a tight bound. The core is a two-split scheme built on risk-controlling prediction sets, whose finite-sample transfer bound crosses from the inferred to the true domain with fully estimable slack, supports heterogeneous budgets, and inverts for deployment. Population validity rests on this bound and a controlled simulation; across a thousand resampled calibrations the certificate never violates (a stability result) while marginal control violates the most-contaminated domain in every draw, and soft demotion retains more recall than the strongest calibrated cascade at equal certified contamination. The method replicates across open testbeds including an independent one from public federal regulations, and an LLM-judged downstream probe indicates wrong-authority grounding rises with contamination and falls under control. The layer is frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic.

</details>


### [149] [PolyQ: Codesigning End-to-End Quantization Framework for Scalable Edge CPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14618)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,KyungIn Nam,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyQ 是一种面向CPU的编译/量化协同设计方案，通过激活感知的逐通道比特分配（{2,3,4,8,16}）和编译时布局优化，在用户指定平均比特预算下实现分数比特的CPU部署，显著提升了低比特量化的PPL和硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU是设备端LLM推理最通用的目标，但现有低比特量化方法要么只能提供粗粒度的工作点，要么提供难以在CPU上高效执行的细粒度混合精度方案，缺乏一种既能精细拟合比特预算又能在CPU上高效执行的实用方法。

Method: PolyQ 由三部分组成：1) 激活感知的逐通道比特分配（位宽选择{2,3,4,8,16}）；2) 编译时模型编译器，将通道置换并聚类为比特同质块，生成SIMD和LUT兼容的内核；3) 跨算子合并兼容的置换操作，将布局正则化移出运行时路径。

Result: 在Falcon-H1-3B、Llama2-13B和Qwen3-32B上，PolyQ在3-6b范围内提供稳定的质量缩放，在3b目标下PPL相比先前方法改进2.4-32.1%。在三种CPU（工作站、笔记本、移动端）上，编译时布局正则化减少激活重排流量高达70.8%，prefill延迟和解码吞吐量与配置的比特预算近似线性缩放，每个token的能量开销相比优化后的LUT后端低于2%。

Conclusion: 分数比特的CPU部署在多样化边缘目标上是实用、可预测且能效高的。

Abstract: CPUs are the most universal target for on-device LLM inference, but existing low-bit quantization methods offer either coarse operating points or fine-grained mixed precision that is difficult to execute efficiently on CPUs. We present PolyQ, a CPU-oriented compiler/quantization co-design for activation-aware channel-wise bit allocation under a user-specified average-bit budget. PolyQ assigns per-channel bit-widths from $\{2,3,4,8,16\}$, then uses a compile-time model compiler to permute and cluster channels into bit-homogeneous blocks, generate SIMD- and LUT-compatible kernels, and merge compatible permutations across operators to keep layout regularization off the runtime path. This turns fine-grained budget fitting into a practical fractional-bit deployment method for CPU-only inference. Across Falcon-H1-3B, Llama2-13B, and Qwen3-32B on WikiText-2, PolyQ provides stable quality scaling from 3--6\,b and improves perplexity by 2.4--32.1\% over prior methods at a 3\,b target. End-to-end measurements on three representative CPUs -- workstation, laptop, and mobile -- show that compiler layout regularization reduces activation reorder traffic by up to 70.8\%, prefill latency and decode throughput scale nearly proportionally with the configured bit budget, and energy/token overhead stays below 2\% relative to an optimized LUT-based back-end. These results show that fractional-bit CPU deployment is practical, predictable, and energy-efficient across diverse edge targets.

</details>


### [150] [QFireNet: A Quantum-Enhanced U-Net for Wildfire Segmentation from Sentinel-2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14160)
*Jaiman Munshi,Tanvi Tewary,Sawyer Bloom,Aidan Chu,Chetan Maviti,Kyon Winston-Bey,Harshit Badjatia,Farhan Kittur,Vardhan Madhavarapu,Varun Kota,Joshua Kwon,Nazia Rangwala-Vohra,Franz Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于U-Net图像分割模型，在瓶颈层注入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net两种ansatz），提出量子-混合解决方案用于卫星图像野火检测。在Sen2Fire数据集上，QB-Net（F1=31.18）和QuFeX（F1=30.79）均优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71），经典FPN达到31.13。数据混合消除了地理域偏移，将FPN的F1提升至39.76。研究验证了量子机器学习在野火图像分割中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像野火检测面临类不平衡、特征复杂和大气干扰等挑战，传统深度学习方法在高维光谱特征空间中建模能力有限，作者希望探索量子-混合方法以更有效地建模高维特征空间，提升野火检测性能。

Method: 在U-Net分割模型的瓶颈层注入变分量子电路（VQC），分别采用QuFeX和QB-Net两种ansatz；同时构建经典FPN作为对比。此外探索了参数压缩、替代损失函数和数据均匀混合等经典改进策略。在Sen2Fire数据集上训练，并在CaBuAr数据集上进行跨数据集迁移验证。

Result: QB-Net（F1=31.18）和QuFeX（F1=30.79）均优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71）；经典FPN得分31.13；数据混合消除域偏移后FPN提升至39.76。跨数据集迁移验证了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习在野火图像分割任务中展现出优于经典基线的潜力，量子-混合方法能够更有效地建模高维光谱特征空间，进一步实验将继续验证和扩展这一发现。

Abstract: Wildfire detection from satellite imagery is a semantic image segmentation problem that has proven to be difficult due to challenges such as class imbalance, feature complexity, and atmospheric interference. In this paper, we build on the foundational U-Net image segmentation model to develop a quantum-hybrid solution in hopes of more effectively modeling the high-dimensional spectral feature space of the Sen2Fire dataset. We inject a variational quantum circuit in the bottleneck portion of U-Net, specifically the QuFeX and QB-Net ansatzes. We test a classical Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) for further comparative analysis of the model, and we also explore classical improvements to the U-Net model and its training process, including a compression of parameters, alternative loss functions, and uniform mixing of input data. Our primary finding is that under matched conditions, both QB-Net (with an $F_1$ score of 31.18) and QuFeX ($F_1 = 30.79$) outperformed the classical U-Net baseline results ($F_1 = 28.71$). Additionally, the classical FPN achieved a comparable score of 31.13. A crucial finding was that data mixing removed a significant domain shift between the geographically-separated train and test sets, which boosted the classical FPN $F_1$ score to 39.76. We validate the architecture's robustness and generalizability to the wildfire detection problem via cross-dataset transfer on the California Burned Areas (CaBuAr) dataset. Overall, we find that quantum machine learning has potential to provide an advantage in the problem of wildfire image segmentation, and further experiments will continue to validate and expand upon this finding.

</details>


### [151] [Branching Policy Optimization: Sandbox-Native Language Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14171)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BPO利用沙箱可快照、可恢复的特性，通过自适应分支共享前缀轨迹来替代传统的独立采样，在减少方差的同时提升了LLM智能体在交互环境中的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法（PPO、RLOO、GRPO）沿袭RLHF的独立轨迹采样拓扑，忽略了沙箱环境确定、可快照、可恢复的特性，导致方差高、样本效率低。

Method: 提出BPO算法：(i) 沿主干轨迹在高熵决策点自适应快照；(ii) 每个分支点分叉K个替代动作并各自 rollout 到终止；(iii) 使用同辈回报计算逐步优势函数。理论上证明了该估计器无偏且方差严格低于轨迹级基线。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld、SWE-bench Verified上，使用Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B，BPO相比GRPO和RLOO在等计算量下提升3.6-6.1绝对点成功率，梯度范数方差减半，且用38%更少的策略更新达到最佳基线水平。

Conclusion: BPO证明了利用沙箱原生特性设计RL拓扑（分支而非独立采样）的有效性，为LLM智能体训练提供了方差更低、样本更高效的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as the dominant paradigm for training large language model (LLM) agents that interact with executable sandboxes. State-of-the-art algorithms such as PPO, RLOO, and GRPO inherit their rollout topology from RLHF: for each prompt, N independent trajectories are sampled from the initial state, and an advantage is computed by subtracting a group baseline. This design ignores a defining property of agent sandboxes. They are deterministic, snapshottable, and resumable from any intermediate state. We argue that this property enables a fundamentally different rollout topology: rather than N independent trees of depth T, one can construct a single tree of N leaves whose siblings share prefixes, and therefore share variance. We instantiate this idea as Branching Policy Optimization (BPO), a sandbox-native RL algorithm that (i) adaptively snapshots the sandbox at high-entropy decision points along a backbone trajectory, (ii) forks K alternative actions per branch point and rolls out each to termination, and (iii) computes per-step advantages from sibling returns rather than from independent prompts. We prove this estimator is unbiased and has strictly lower variance than the trajectory-level baseline, with the reduction equal to the prefix-explained portion of return variance. On WebShop, ALFWorld, and SWE-bench Verified with Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B backbones, BPO improves success by 3.6--6.1 absolute points over GRPO and RLOO at matched compute, halves gradient-norm variance, and matches the best baseline using 38% fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [152] [How Much of a 10-K Matters? Aggregation-Dependent Value of Full-Text versus Risk-Factor Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14174)
*Sanggyu Sean Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从10-K年报（特别是Item 1A风险因素部分）中提取金融情感信息的方法，比较了有监督学习与词典基线的效果，发现全文本在行业和组合层面表现更好，而Item 1A在单个公司层面更优，并验证了有监督方法对监管披露文本的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融情感提取长期依赖新闻文本和针对收益率标签的有监督方法，而对10-K年报文件以及波动性（风险披露最适合预测的目标）关注不足，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将有监督词典学习方法扩展到10-K年报及其Item 1A风险因素部分，针对收益率和波动性标签在行业、组合和单个公司三个聚合层级训练情感得分，使用1,383份Nasdaq-100科技公司文件（2006–2023）进行评估。

Result: 全文本在行业和组合层面对两个目标的情感准确性更高，但在单个公司层面Item 1A表现更好；Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层级上与价格呈强烈负相关，凸显了有监督方法的优势。

Conclusion: 本文发现和方法设计选择为后续更大规模、多源系统奠定了情感生成方法论基础，并验证了有监督学习在监管披露文本中的价值。

Abstract: Financial sentiment extraction has largely relied on news text and supervised extraction against return labels alone, leaving 10-K filings -- and volatility, the target risk disclosure is arguably best suited to informing -- comparatively unexplored. We extend a supervised lexicon-learning approach to 10-K filings and their Item 1A risk-factor sections, training sentiment scores against both return and volatility labels at three levels of aggregation: sector, portfolio, and individual firm. Across 1,383 filings from 94 Nasdaq-100 technology constituents (2006--2023), we evaluate the resulting twelve sentiment metrics on classification accuracy, correlation with realised market outcomes, and qualitative lexical content. Full-filing text produces more accurate sentiment at the sector and portfolio level for both targets, but this reverses at the individual-firm level, where the narrower Item 1A section performs better -- an effect we attribute to the interaction between document volume and the amount of independent training signal available at each level of aggregation. A Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline is consistently, strongly negatively correlated with price at every level tested, underscoring the value of a supervised approach for regulatory disclosure text. These findings, and the design choices they motivate, establish the sentiment-generation methodology underlying a subsequent, larger-scale, multi-source system.

</details>


### [153] [Low-Latency Relay Selection in NR-V2X Vehicular Communications via Graph Isomorphism Networks with Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14176)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini,Pierpaolo Salvo,Paola Vocca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图同构网络（GINE）的边缘感知学习优化框架，用于C-V2X网络中实时中继选择。通过离线MILP最优解监督训练GINE模型，实现单次前向传播的边级中继激活，推理延迟严格控制在5ms以内。进一步提出GP-MILP混合策略，利用GINE预测剪枝MILP搜索空间，在98%以上图实例中实现30ms内求解且保持MILP等效解。实验表明，链路级准确率0.9589，F1-score 0.9544，相比1跳MILP基线在端到端连接性上提升高达12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C-V2X网络在密集城市环境中需要可靠、低延迟的上行连接，但快速信道变化和阻塞会降低直连V2I链路性能。多跳中继可恢复覆盖，但受限于无线、容量和路由约束的中继链路激活是NP-hard问题，传统MILP求解规模扩展性差，无法满足NR-V2X的严格延迟预算。

Method: 将每个V2X快照建模为有向图，节点特征编码车辆状态和流量需求，边特征编码无线链路容量。使用离线MILP Oracle生成最优中继配置作为标签，训练带有边特征的图同构网络（GINE），实现边级中继激活的单次前向推理。进一步提出GP-MILP混合策略，用GINE预测剪枝MILP搜索空间以加速求解。

Result: 在OSM-SUMO-GEMV^2管道生成的大规模数据集上，GINE链路级准确率0.9589，F1-score 0.9544；相比1跳MILP基线，端到端连接性提升达9.2%（4个RSU）和12%（2个RSU）；推理延迟始终低于5ms；GP-MILP在98%以上图实例中30ms内求解且保持MILP等效目标值。

Conclusion: 所提边缘感知学习优化框架实现了MILP级优化质量与实时推理能力的兼顾，GINE单次前向传播满足NR-V2X延迟约束，GP-MILP混合策略进一步在严格延迟预算内提供MILP等效解，为密集城市环境下C-V2X中继选择提供可行方案。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency uplink connectivity is a key requirement for C-V2X networks in dense urban environments, where fast channel variations and blockages often degrade direct vehicle-to-infrastructure links. Multi-hop relaying can restore coverage, but relay-link activation under radio, capacity, and routing constraints results in an NP-hard optimisation problem, typically solved via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), whose runtime scales poorly with graph size. This paper introduces an edge-aware Learning-to-Optimise framework for real-time relay selection. Each V2X snapshot is modelled as a directed graph: node features encode vehicle state and traffic demand, while edge features capture radio-link capacity. An offline MILP oracle generates optimal relay configurations that supervise a Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE), enabling edge-level relay activation through a single forward pass, with tightly bounded inference latency. To bridge learning and exact optimisation, we also propose a hybrid GINE-Pruned MILP (GP-MILP) strategy in which GINE predictions prune the MILP search space. Experiments on a large-scale dataset generated via an OSM-SUMO-GEMV$^2$ pipeline show that GINE closely matches MILP decisions at the link level (accuracy 0.9589), F1-score (0.9544) on validation) and yields consistent end-to-end connectivity gains over a 1-hop MILP baseline (up to 9.2% with four RSUs and 12% with two RSUs). Inference latency remains tightly bounded, with all evaluated instances completing within 5~ms. Moreover, GP-MILP preserves MILP-equivalent solutions (same objective value) while achieving solver runtimes below 30~ms for more than 98%) of the graph instances, making MILP-grade optimisation compatible with stringent NR-V2X latency budgets.

</details>


### [154] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongStraw是一个面向百万级token RL后训练的架构感知执行栈，通过共享提示词不保留梯度、按需回放短响应分支等策略，在固定GPU预算下实现超长上下文的后训练，在8块H20上支持210万位置的分组评分与反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理上下文长度（百万级）与RL后训练长度（通常≤256K）之间存在日益扩大的鸿沟，导致部署时依赖长度泛化。这对AI agent尤为关键，因为其长轨迹中累积了观察、工具输出、文档和先前的决策。

Method: LongStraw执行栈的核心策略包括：对共享提示词进行评估时不启用autograd；仅保留后续token所需的特定模型状态；将短响应分支逐个回放，从而缩小实时训练图，以额外的回放时间换取显存开销。在Qwen3.6-27B（混合循环+全注意力）和GLM-5.2（压缩注意力的MoE）上实现。

Result: 在8块H20 GPU上，LongStraw支持分组为2和8的210万位置的分组Qwen评分与响应反向传播；分组大小增加仅增加0.21 GB峰值显存。在32块H20上验证了GLM-5.2全部78层的210万token提示词的端到端执行路径。压力测试达到446万位置。

Conclusion: 实验验证了执行容量而非完整的训练正确性，因为捕获的提示词状态是分离的，部分分布式前向和梯度组合路径仍不完整。LongStraw为弥合推理与后训练之间的上下文长度鸿沟提供了可行的系统级方案。

Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

</details>


### [155] [RENEW: Towards Learning World Models and Repairing Model Exploitation from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14180)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Mykel Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从人类偏好中直接修复世界模型的动态学习问题（DLHF），并引入RENEW方法通过 epistemic uncertainty 提高样本效率，减少离线模型强化学习中的模型利用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中的世界模型在数据覆盖稀疏时容易遭受模型利用（model exploitation），先前方法要么依赖昂贵/不安全的专家数据收集，要么采用保守算法限制泛化能力。本文旨在通过人类偏好直接修复模型利用问题，利用人类直觉物理能力识别动态幻觉。

Method: 提出 Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF)，使用 Bradley-Terry 偏好损失对学习到的动力学模型下的轨迹对数似然进行优化。进一步提出 RENEW，利用 epistemic uncertainty 将微调集中在模型最易被利用的区域，提升样本效率。

Result: 在 Jumanji 和经典控制环境中，朴素 DLHF 需要大量偏好预算，而 RENEW 显著提升了样本效率，限制了灾难性遗忘，并减少了预训练世界模型中的模型利用问题。

Conclusion: 人类偏好可以直接监督世界模型的动态学习，为解决离线模型强化学习中的模型利用问题提供了一种新途径。

Abstract: World models are widely used in offline reinforcement learning (RL) to improve sample efficiency and generate experience beyond a fixed dataset. However, they are vulnerable to model exploitation where data coverage is thin. Prior work addresses this either by collecting more expert demonstrations, which is often expensive, unsafe, or unavailable, or by conservative algorithms that avoid uncertain regions, which limits generalization. We propose instead to repair exploitation directly using human preferences over imagined rollouts, leveraging the strong intuitive physics that allows humans to easily spot egregious dynamics hallucinations. We formalize this as Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF), a Bradley-Terry preference loss over trajectory log-likelihoods under a learned dynamics model. Unfortunately, naive DLHF is sample inefficient, so we introduce RENEW, which uses epistemic uncertainty to focus finetuning where the model is most exploitable. We evaluate on several Jumanji and classic control environments and find that while naive DLHF requires an outsize preference budget, RENEW makes the framework practical by improving sample efficiency, limiting catastrophic forgetting, and reducing exploitation in pretrained world models. Taken together, our results provide initial evidence that preferences can supervise world model dynamics directly, offering a new approach to addressing exploitation in offline model-based RL.

</details>


### [156] [Closed-Loop Knowledge Dynamics: An Operational Framework for Saturation and Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14185)
*Xuening Wu,Shan Yu,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了闭环知识系统在重复内部反馈下性能饱和的原因，提出了一个三层操作框架来解释知识状态的演化与吸引子行为，并通过Lyapunov漂移条件、KL下界等工具分析了结构干预如何使系统逃离当前吸引子。案例涵盖LLM代码修复、稀疏奖励强化学习和贝叶斯优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈驱动的迭代改进（如LLM、RL、自主发现）在重复内部反馈下增益递减，现有方法难以突破性能饱和。本文旨在理解知识系统为何饱和，以及何种外部信息能帮助系统逃离当前吸引子。

Method: 提出三层操作框架：知识状态x_t通过转移核K_θ演化；定义θ的观测等价类作为结构；吸引子和盆地由固定θ动力学决定。使用Lyapunov漂移条件证明稳定内动态趋近有界稳定区域，并给出逃逸的度量条件：干预引起的吸引子位移的度量条件，以及基于KL下界的逃逸概率上界。通过条件互信息分析说明其不能保证逃逸。

Result: 证明了稳定内动态会逼近有界稳定区域，瞬态指数衰减，噪声控制残余误差。给出了结构干预可检测的条件（在探针状态上产生核差异），以及逃逸概率的KL下界。解释了条件互信息不能认证逃逸的原因。案例研究验证了反馈强度和一致性对质量提升逃逸的影响。

Conclusion: 建立了稳定性工具、可测量干预效应与跨领域诊断之间的操作化连接，为理解和设计有效的知识系统干预策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Feedback-driven loops support iterative improvement in large language models, reinforcement learning, and autonomous discovery, yet their gains often diminish under repeated internal feedback. We study why closed-loop knowledge systems saturate and what external information can move them beyond their current attractors. We introduce a three-level operational framework in which knowledge states $x_t$ evolve through transition kernels $K_θ$ indexed by a structural parameter $θ$. The governing structure is defined as the observational equivalence class of $θ$ induced by these kernels, while attractors and basins are properties of the fixed-$θ$ dynamics. A structural intervention changes $θ$ and produces a detectable kernel discrepancy on pre-specified probe states, making structural change falsifiable. Using a Lyapunov drift condition, we show that stable internal dynamics approach bounded stability regions with exponentially attenuated transients and a noise-controlled residual floor. We characterize escape through a metric condition on intervention-induced attractor displacement and a baseline-relative KL lower bound for increasing escape probability. This analysis also explains why conditional mutual information alone cannot certify escape: it measures variation among intervention-conditioned updates rather than departure from the no-intervention law. Case studies in LLM code repair, sparse-reward reinforcement learning, and Bayesian optimization use matched continuation controls to illustrate how feedback strength and alignment affect quality-improving escape. Our contribution is an operational connection among stability tools, measurable intervention effects, and cross-domain diagnostics.

</details>


### [157] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数字孪生启发的时间-事件预测框架，通过整合纵向ALSFRS-R轨迹与生存建模，实现肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者功能衰退及辅助设备使用的个体化预测。该框架采用相关性聚类识别功能域、广义加性混合模型刻画非线性衰退、时序机器学习预测纵向进展、Cox比例风险模型识别轮椅使用关键预测因子，最终生成个体化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS是一种进展性、异质性强的神经退行性疾病，预测辅助设备使用等临床里程碑具有挑战性，现有方法缺乏个体化、动态化的预测能力。

Method: 1. 构建包含诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动及人口统计学信息的纵向数据集，经预处理确保数据质量与时间对齐；2. 基于相关性聚类识别球部、上肢、轴向、下肢、呼吸系统等功能域；3. 采用广义加性混合模型刻画各功能域的非线性衰退轨迹；4. 开发时序机器学习模型预测纵向功能衰退及疾病阶段性进展；5. 使用Cox比例风险模型识别下肢功能（步行、爬楼梯）为轮椅使用的关键预测因子；6. 构建数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件模型，生成个体化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期。

Result: 下肢功能（特别是步行和爬楼梯）是早期轮椅使用的最强预测因子；该框架能生成个体化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期，具有可扩展性、可解释性和临床可操作性。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件框架为ALS进展预测与个性化决策支持提供了可扩展、可解释的解决方案，可应用于主动护理规划、临床试验分层及精准医学。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [158] [TEDDY: A Pediatric Foundation Model for Risk Forewarning from ICD-Coded Diagnostic Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14191)
*Matthew Brady Neeley,Jorge Botas,Johnathan Jia,Lin Yao,Daniel Palacios,Benjamin Choi,Zhandong Liu,Hyun-Hwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEDDY是一个仅184万参数的解码器Transformer模型，在160万儿童的约7300万ICD-10诊断记录上训练，能够建模儿童纵向诊断轨迹和就诊时间。在797项疾病发病预测任务中取得中位AUC 72.0%，显著优于DenseNet、CNN、RNN、LSTM等基线模型，并在罕见病预测、提前两年以上的长期预测中表现有效，证明儿童诊断历史可作为紧凑生成模型的基板，无需大规模数据或十亿级参数即可支持广泛、罕见病及远期风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然儿童电子健康记录包含结构化的发育纵向临床轨迹，但其在生成式医疗基础模型中的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用1.84M参数的解码器Transformer（TEDDY），在约7300万条ICD-10诊断记录（来自160万儿童）上训练，建模纵向诊断轨迹和就诊时间。预测采用逐次展开的方式（在就诊代码揭示前进行预测），限制为首次发病预测，并与性别和年龄匹配的对照进行对比评估。

Result: 在16个ICD-10章节的797项发病预测任务中，TEDDY中位AUC为72.0%，在96-99%任务上优于基线模型。在225种最罕见疾病中，90%的95%置信区间高于随机水平。预测信号在首次诊断记录前两年以上即可检测到（中位AUC 59.7%）。哮喘和ADHD预测AUC达79.3%和84.7%，远超最强对比模型（62.7%和71.7%），包括体积大三个数量级的通用语言模型。就诊时间预测在365天内的平均绝对受限平均生存时间误差为3.0天。

Conclusion: 儿科诊断历史可用于构建紧凑的生成式模型，无需十亿级参数或大规模数据即可实现广泛疾病、罕见病和远期风险的预测。

Abstract: Pediatric electronic health records capture developmentally structured clinical trajectories, yet their potential for generative healthcare foundation models remains largely unexplored. Here we present TEDDY (Temporal Event Decoder for Disease in Youth), a 1.84-million-parameter decoder transformer trained on approximately 73 million ICD-10 diagnoses from 1.6 million children at a single pediatric institution. TEDDY models longitudinal diagnosis trajectories and visit timing. Predictions were made before visit codes were revealed, limited to first occurrences, and evaluated against sex- and age-matched controls. Across 797 disease-onset prediction tasks spanning 16 ICD-10 chapters, TEDDY achieved a median AUC of 72.0%, outperforming same-data DenseNet (50.0%), CNN (57.2%), RNN (60.1%), and LSTM (62.7%) baselines on 96-99% of tasks. Performance held across sex and age and was strongest among lower-prevalence diagnoses; 202 of the 225 rarest conditions (90%) had 95% confidence intervals above chance. Predictive signal remained detectable more than two years before first recorded diagnosis, with median AUCs of 59.7% in the unrestricted analysis and 64.4% in a fixed-cohort sensitivity analysis. In asthma and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder benchmarks, AUCs were 79.3% and 84.7%, compared with 62.7% and 71.7% for the strongest comparators, including a general-purpose language model three orders of magnitude larger. Visit-timing predictions had a 3.0-day mean absolute restricted mean survival-time error over 365 days, although median and long-tail return intervals remained miscalibrated. Together, these results establish pediatric diagnostic histories as a substrate for compact generative models supporting broad, rare-disease, and long-horizon risk forecasting without population-scale data or billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [159] [Long-term User Engagement Optimization through Model-agnostic Downstream Rewards Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14192)
*Dingsu Wang,Filip Ryzner,Kelly He,Armando Ordorica,David Woo,Aditya Mantha,Liyao Lu,Usha Amrutha Nookala,Haoran Guo,Jiacong He,Olafur Gudmundsson,Matt Chun,Krystal Benitez,Dhruvil Deven Badani,Yijie Dylan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一且与模型无关的下游奖励框架，用于优化大规模推荐系统中的长期用户价值。该框架通过离线筛选识别可早期观测且预测留存率的会话级行为，生成多种下游奖励信号，并成功在Pinterest多个推荐场景（首页、相关图钉、搜索、通知）部署，A/B实验证明能持续提升用户参与度和留存率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的优化目标已从短期行为信号转向长期用户留存，但直接优化留存面临信号稀疏、延迟且仅部分可归因于推荐行为的挑战。现有方法（如序列建模和强化学习）需要任务特定的奖励工程、计算开销大且难以泛化。

Method: 首先形式化下游奖励学习问题，建立离线筛选框架识别早期可观测且预测留存率的会话级行为；然后提出多种与模型无关的下游奖励信号，这些信号来源于跨多个来源的用户行为模式；最后讨论生产化部署的工程挑战及解决方案。

Result: 在线A/B实验证明该框架在用户参与度和留存相关指标上均有持续改进，并已成功部署到Pinterest的Homefeed、Related Pins、Search和Notifications等多个推荐场景。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一、模型无关且可泛化的框架，有效解决了长期用户价值优化的稀疏奖励和延迟反馈问题，在大规模生产系统中取得了实际业务指标的提升。

Abstract: As recommender systems mature in the past few years, their optimization objectives have evolved from a primary focusing on short-term behavioral signals to a broader emphasis on long-term user engagement and retention. However, directly optimizing retention is difficult because return signals are sparse, delayed, and only partially attributable to earlier recommendations. Prior work has addressed this challenge with sequential modeling and reinforcement learning, but these approaches typically require task specific reward engineering, substantial computational overhead, and surface specific implementations that are difficult to generalize. In this paper, we present a unified, model-agnostic downstream reward framework for optimizing long-term user value in large-scale recommendation systems. First, we formulate the downstream reward learning problem and develop an offline screening framework to identify session level behaviors that are both observable early and predictive of future retention. We then propose several model-agnostic downstream rewards signals derived from observed user action patterns across multiple sources. We further discuss the engineering effort to productionize the proposed rewards derivations and challenges we faced when adding them to our ranking models. Online A/B experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in engagement and retention-related metrics, and the framework has been deployed across multiple Pinterest surfaces, including Homefeed, Related Pins, Search, and Notifications.

</details>


### [160] [Augmentations for Robust and Efficient Imitation Learning in Streamed Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14200)
*Somjit Nath,Abdelhak Lemkhenter,Pallavi Choudhury,Chris Lovett,Katja Hofmann,Sergio Valcarcel Macua,Lukas Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对流媒体环境的时空数据增强方法，通过模拟低带宽网络下常见的四种视觉伪影（像素块、划痕、全局模糊和鬼影），有效提升了基于逆动力学模型的游戏代理在流媒体条件下的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习依赖人类演示，但演示数据收集成本高；现代游戏常通过流媒体进行，网络延迟和压缩引入的时空相关视觉伪影会导致测试时的协变量偏移，降低代理性能。

Method: 提出流媒体增强方法，模拟低带宽网络下的四种伪影：像素块与划痕、全局模糊、鬼影；将增强集成到预测性逆动力学模型（PIDM）中，结合未来状态条件与逆动力学策略在潜在空间进行学习。

Result: 在稳定流媒体条件下，使用增强的代理在相同数据预算下性能提升高达41%；引入网络延迟时，增强代理性能仅下降7.45%，而未增强代理下降49.82%。

Conclusion: 针对流媒体场景设计的时空增强是一种简单但有效的方法，能够训练出鲁棒且高效的游戏代理。

Abstract: Imitation learning is an appealing way to scale game-playing agents to complex 3D environments by training policies to map visual observations to actions from human demonstrations. However, these demonstrations are expensive to collect and modern game-playing is often done through streaming in which network delay and compression introduce spatiotemporally correlated visual artifacts that can cause a covariance shift at test time. To address these challenges, we propose streaming augmentations that mimic four types of artifacts commonly encountered during streaming with low-bandwidth network connection: pixelated blocks and scrubs, global blur, and ghosting. We instantiate our approach on top of predictive inverse dynamics models (PIDM), which combine future-state conditioning with an inverse dynamics policy in a learned latent space, and evaluate the impact of our augmentations across three tasks in modern 3D video games. Under stable streaming conditions, agents trained with spatiotemporal augmentations achieve up to 41% higher evaluation performance compared to agents trained without augmentations under an identical data budget. When network lag is introduced, agents trained with augmentations degrade by only 7.45% vs 49.82% of the original performance for agents trained only with the original data. These results clearly indicate that spatiotemporal augmentations tailored for the streaming setting are a simple yet powerful tool to train robust and efficient game-playing agents.

</details>


### [161] [Privacy Leakage in Federated Learning in Radiology Reports: A Comparative Evaluation of Tokenizer-Driven Privacy Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14205)
*Santhosh Parampottupadam,Andres Martinez,Dimitrios Bounias,Sinem Sav,Klaus Maier-Hein,Ralf Floca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 联邦学习（FL）中，即使使用领域专用分词器和大批量大小，放射学报告文本仍可从梯度更新中高精度重建（句子重建率31%-44%），分词器设计显著影响隐私泄露程度，RadBERT泄露最严重（恢复18.1%临床术语），现有FL机制无法满足HIPAA/GDPR合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告包含高度敏感的患者健康信息，联邦学习虽可避免直接共享原始数据，但梯度反演攻击可能泄露模型更新中的隐私信息。目前尚不清楚：（1）放射学报告在FL中的梯度隐私泄露程度有多严重；（2）分词器设计（通用vs.领域专用）如何影响这种隐私风险。量化这些风险对于评估FL在医学NLP中的合规性至关重要。

Method: 在固定GPT-2风格Transformer架构（序列长度32）下，对比三种分词器（GPT-2、RadBERT、LLaMA-2）的隐私风险。六个FL客户端在三个公开放射学语料库（368,751份诊断报告、98,206份出院小结、1,500份MIMIC-CXR自由文本报告）上训练，批量大小为64、128、256。假设恶意服务器在分发前修改共享架构，应用解析梯度反演攻击，测量五次运行的重建保真度。

Result: 精确句子重建率在31%-44%之间（27个条件组合下为30.6%-43.5%）。批量大小64时，出院数据集重建准确率：GPT-2为42.1%、RadBERT为42.3%、LLaMA-2为39.4%；批量大小256时分别降至37.3%、37.2%、34.3%。S-BLEU随批量增大而下降（GPT-2: 0.44→0.33；RadBERT: 0.48→0.35）。RadBERT重建保真度最高，恢复临床术语最多（18.1%参考词汇表，GPT-2为12.5%，LLaMA-2为9.4%）。没有任何分词器能完全阻止泄露。

Conclusion: 大量报告文本可从FL梯度中恢复，即使使用较大批量大小和领域专用分词器。分词器设计是影响隐私泄露严重程度的关键决策，而不仅仅是影响模型效用的因素。现有FL设置不足以满足HIPAA和GDPR的隐私要求，必须采用安全聚合和差分隐私等保护机制。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multi-institutional training on clinical text without sharing raw data, but gradient inversion can reconstruct sensitive information from shared model updates. The extent of this leakage for radiology reports, and the role of tokenizer design, remains unclear. We quantify gradient-based text reconstruction in FL and compare privacy risk across three tokenizers with the model architecture held fixed. Six FL clients trained a GPT-2-style transformer (sequence length 32) on public radiology corpora (368,751 diagnostic reports, 98,206 discharge summaries, 1,500 MIMIC-CXR free-text reports) using the GPT-2, RadBERT, and LLaMA-2 tokenizers at batch sizes of 64, 128, and 256. Assuming an active malicious server that modifies the shared architecture before distribution, we applied analytic gradient inversion and measured reconstruction fidelity over five runs. Exact sentence reconstruction ranged from 31% to 44% across tokenizers (30.6-43.5% across the 27 tokenizer x dataset x batch-size cells). At batch size 64 on the Discharge dataset, accuracy was 42.1% (GPT-2), 42.3% (RadBERT), and 39.4% (LLaMA-2), decreasing to 37.3%, 37.2%, and 34.3% at batch size 256. S-BLEU declined as batch size grew (GPT-2: 0.44 to 0.33; RadBERT: 0.48 to 0.35). RadBERT yielded the highest reconstruction fidelity and recovered the most clinical terms (18.1% of a 1,440-term reference vocabulary, vs 12.5% for GPT-2 and 9.4% for LLaMA-2), yet no tokenizer prevented leakage. Substantial portions of report text are therefore recoverable from FL gradients even at larger batch sizes and with domain-specific tokenizers. Tokenizer design influences leakage severity and is a privacy-relevant decision, not only a utility one; safeguards such as secure aggregation and differential privacy are likely necessary to meet HIPAA and GDPR requirements for FL in radiology NLP.

</details>


### [162] [LIGO-PINN: Learned Initialization via Gated Optimization to Alleviate Convergence Failures in Physics Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14233)
*Nilay Anurag,Shital Adhikari,Taniya Kapoor,Nikhil Muralidhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LIGO-PINN框架，通过学习的门控逐层初始化策略（Learned Initialization via Gated Layerwise Optimization）来解决物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在偏微分方程建模中的收敛失败问题，在多个PDE基准上平均性能提升91.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在复杂PDE领域或泛化到未见过的相关PDE领域时表现不佳，甚至收敛到平凡解。现有解决方案（超参数调优、课程学习、动态重采样）存在各自缺陷，而PINN权重初始化的影响却被显著忽视了。

Method: 提出LIGO-PINN框架，通过门控逐层优化学习初始化权重的策略，为PINN提供更好的初始网络参数，从而防止训练过程中的灾难性收敛失败。

Result: 在1D和2D PDE域（含挑战性2D流体动力学）上，平均性能比六个基线方法提升91.5%，比最强基线提升81%；并在3D非结构网格上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: PINN的初始网络权重对训练成败至关重要，LIGO-PINN通过学习的初始化策略有效克服了收敛失败问题，在多个PDE领域取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have had a broad research impact in modeling domains governed by partial differential equations (PDE). However, PINNs have been shown to perform poorly, sometimes even converging to trivial solutions, in challenging PDE domains, or when generalizing to unseen but related PDE domains. Previously proposed solutions detail hyperparameter tuning to reduce loss imbalance between data-driven and physics guided losses, curriculum learning based training strategies, or dynamic re-sampling of hard collocation points. These methods face certain pitfalls: hyperparameter tuning is expensive, designing a training curriculum is ambiguous in multi-parameter PDE settings, and dynamic resampling still fails in complex PDE settings. Complementary to this line of thinking, we believe the initial PINN network weights also play a crucial role in the emergence of catastrophic failures during training, yet the effect of PINN weight initialization has been surprisingly under-investigated. To this end, we propose a framework for Learned Initialization via Gated Layerwise Optimization (LIGO-PINN) to overcome PINN convergence failures. Through rigorous evaluation on 1D and 2D PDE domains, including a challenging 2D fluid dynamics setting, we demonstrate that our methodology outperforms state-of-the-art methods designed to alleviate PINN failures, achieving a 91.5% average performance improvement across six baselines and 81% over the strongest baseline. We also verify that LIGO-PINN generalizes to 3D unstructured domains. Finally, we analyze training dynamics across all three PDE domains to explain both LIGO-PINN's improvement and the convergence failure of traditional PINNs. Code: https://github.com/scailab/ligo-pinn
  Keywords: Machine Learning, Physics-Informed Neural Networks, Deep Learning, PDE Modeling

</details>


### [163] [MIDiff: Tackling Sparsity and Imbalance in Mobile Usage Generation via Multivariate-Imaging Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14249)
*Yilai Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MIDiff，一种基于扩散模型的移动使用轨迹生成框架。通过C-GASF将稀疏多元序列转换为相关图像，并利用Unet中的三重注意力机制保持时间一致性和变量依赖性。在保真度指标上达到SOTA，DA指标为0.1526，远超基线ZITS-VAE的0.3476。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动使用轨迹因隐私限制和大规模数据收集成本高昂而受限。现有生成模型在处理移动使用数据时面临三大挑战：(i)用户活动有限导致严重稀疏性，(ii)异构变量类型使联合建模复杂化，(iii)不同App的功能差异导致使用不平衡。

Method: 提出Multivariate-Imaging Diffusion (MIDiff)框架。首先通过Cross-Gramian Angular Sum Field (C-GASF)将稀疏多元序列转换为相关图像，将问题转化为图像生成任务。然后在U-Net中采用Triple Attention机制，在扩散过程的去噪阶段保持时间一致性和变量依赖关系。

Result: 在保真度指标上达到最先进性能。Discriminative Accuracy (DA)为0.1526，而最强基线ZITS-VAE为0.3476，表明生成的移动使用轨迹更加真实且多样化。

Conclusion: MIDiff有效解决了移动使用轨迹生成中的稀疏性、异构性和不平衡性问题，为隐私受限场景下的移动数据生成提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile usage traces are critical for tasks such as user behavior prediction and app recommendation, yet their use is constrained by privacy restrictions and costly large-scale data collection. Although generative models perform well on general time series, their application to mobile usage data remains challenging because (i) limited user activity causes severe sparsity, (ii) heterogeneous variable types complicate joint modeling, and (iii) functional differences across apps create pronounced usage imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose Multivariate-Imaging Diffusion (MIDiff), a diffusion-based framework operating in an imaging space defined by Cross-Gramian Angular Sum Field (C-GASF). C-GASF transforms sparse multivariate sequences into correlation images, while MIDiff employs Triple Attention in a U-Net to preserve temporal consistency and variable dependencies. Experiments show that MIDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance across fidelity metrics. In particular, it obtains a Discriminative Accuracy (DA) of 0.1526, compared with 0.3476 for the strongest baseline, ZITS-VAE, demonstrating its effectiveness in generating realistic and diverse mobile usage traces. Our code is available at https://github.com/YilaiLiu-HKU/MIDiff.

</details>


### [164] [Local Additive Feature Attribution: A Mathematical Taxonomy and Reporting Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14271)
*Rebecca Afriyie Sarpong,Daniel Commey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对局部加性特征归因方法提出统一框架，通过五个规范选择（价值函数、参考点、路径、扰动分布、守恒规则）组织Shapley值、路径积分、梯度/反向传播、扰动和CAM类方法，并通过公理对比矩阵和失败模式分析揭示归因结果依赖于其数学假设，最后提出十项报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法（Shapley值、路径积分、梯度/反向传播、扰动方法、CAM类方法）分散在不同数学语言中（合作博弈值、路径积分、梯度算子、扰动分布、反向传播规则），缺乏统一的分析框架来比较它们的假设、优势和局限。

Method: 提出五个规范选择作为统一框架：价值函数、参考点、路径、扰动分布和守恒规则；构建方法-公理矩阵；分析常见失败模式与假设之间的关联；提出十项报告检查清单。

Result: 成功将多种局部加性归因方法纳入统一框架，揭示了基线敏感性、离流形扰动、完整性检查失败、对抗操纵和方法不一致等失败模式与其底层假设的因果关系。

Conclusion: 归因结果仅在其定义的数学假设下才有意义，研究者应明确报告这些假设；十项检查清单为可解释AI研究提供了标准化报告规范。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods are central to explainable artificial intelligence. Their assumptions are expressed in several mathematical languages: cooperative-game values, path integrals, gradient operators, perturbation distributions, and backpropagation rules. This survey proposes a common framework for local additive feature attribution. It organizes Shapley, path-based, gradient/backpropagation, perturbation, and CAM-style methods around five specification choices: value function, reference, path, perturbation distribution, and conservation rule. It then compares these methods through an axiom-by-method matrix and links common failure modes, including baseline sensitivity, off-manifold perturbations, sanity-check failures, adversarial manipulation, and method disagreement, to the assumptions that produce them. Finally, the survey proposes a ten-item reporting checklist for studies that use local additive attributions. The central message is that attribution results are meaningful only relative to the mathematical assumptions under which they are defined, and that those assumptions should be reported.

</details>


### [165] [Lyapunov Guidance: A Unified Framework for Stabilizing Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14272)
*Jingdong Zhang,Xinze Li,Yize Jiang,Luan Yang,Minkai Xu,Junhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LyaGuide框架，将流匹配中的引导问题转化为Lyapunov控制问题，通过伪投影算子为引导项提供显式稳定性保证，在多个任务中提升样本质量和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配后训练引导方法多为启发式，缺乏显式的稳定性保证，且重新训练计算开销大。

Method: 将引导流匹配与Lyapunov控制建立理论等价关系，提出具有闭式解的伪投影算子来强制满足Lyapunov条件，支持模型驱动和数据驱动两种设置。

Result: 在合成基准、图像逆问题、强化学习规划和基于能量的建模等任务上，一致提升了样本质量、引导保真度和鲁棒性，且计算开销小。

Conclusion: LyaGuide为流匹配引导提供了统一的理论框架和稳定性保证，兼容现有方法，易于集成，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as an effective framework for learning complex data distributions, but adapting pretrained flow models to new tasks often requires computationally expensive retraining. Post-training guidance provides a more efficient alternative, but existing methods are largely heuristic and offer no explicit stability guarantees. We address this limitation by proposing LyaGuide, a unified Lyapunov-guided framework that formulates flow guidance as a Lyapunov control problem. Our main theoretical result establishes an equivalence between guided flow matching and Lyapunov control, thereby unifying common guidance strategies, such as classifier guidance, reward guidance, and energy-based guidance, within a single control-theoretic framework.
